## Transcripts

### greet-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: 你好，你能帮我做什么？
  [partner message] 我是 Ducter，你的个人 AI 伙伴。比如你可以让我修好某个仓库里挂掉的测试。
```

### greet-en

```
--- step 1: user says: hi! what can you do?
  [partner message] I'm Ducter, your partner for work on your Mac. For example, I can fix a failing test in one of your repos and tell you when it's done.
```

### greet-ko

### direct-es

```
--- step 1: user says: En dos líneas: ¿cuándo debo hacer rebase en vez de merge?
  [partner message] Usa rebase para actualizar tu rama privada con los cambios de la base antes de compartirla, para mantener un historial lineal. Usa merge para integrar una rama ya compartida o publicada, porque reescribir su historial rompe a quienes ya la usan.
```

### direct-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: 一句话：TCP 和 UDP 最大的区别是什么？
  [partner message] TCP 是面向连接、可靠的传输：数据有确认、丢了会重传、按序交付；UDP 是无连接、不保证送达，换来的是更低的延迟和开销。
```

### direct-en

```
--- step 1: user says: In two lines: when should I rebase instead of merge?
  [partner message] Rebase your own unshared branch to bring it up to date and keep history linear before opening a PR. Merge when the branch is already shared or public, since rebasing rewrites history other people may have built on.
```

### direct-zh-howto

```
--- step 1: user says: bun test 怎么只跑一个测试文件？
  [partner message] 直接把文件路径传给 `bun test` 就行：

```bash
bun test test/foo.test.ts
```

也可以只写文件名的一部分，Bun 会匹配路径里包含这段字符的测试文件：

```bash
bun test foo
```

如果还想只跑文件里的某一个测试用例，加 `-t` 再写用例名的一部分：

```bash
bun test test/foo.test.ts -t "某个用例"
```
```

### mem-source-none-en

```
--- step 1: user says: In two sentences: what's a write-ahead log for?
  [partner message] A write-ahead log records every change to a sequential file before the database applies it to the main data. After a crash, the database replays that log to recover committed changes and discard incomplete ones, so data stays consistent without writing every page to disk on each commit.
```

(earlier) partner ack: On it: every night at 23:00.  → task "Nightly hawdb write benchmark" working; its report: Night 3: 182k writes/s, p99 4.1 ms, level with last night (181k, 4.0 ms). Compaction stalls: 0.
(earlier) partner: Night 3: 182k writes/s at p99 4.1 ms, level with last night; no compaction stalls.

--- step 1: user answers the open decision: go
  [partner message] Done: the board is up with Night 2 and Night 3 filled in, and it will add a row each night as the benchmark reports.
  [an app card under the message: made "hawdb nightly write benchmark", with Open] the app's file now (v1, 1599 characters): <main class="k-app">
<div class="k-sum">
  <div class="k-sum-label">Latest writes/s</div>
  <div class="k-sum-value" id="w"><span class="k-none">Not yet</span></div>
  <div class="k-sum-sub" id="s">hawdb write benchmark, every night at 23:00</div>
</div>
<h2 class="k-h">Nights</h2>
<div class="k-card"><div class="k-list" id="list"></div></div>
</main>
<script>
function fmt(n){return (typeof n==='number')?n.toLocaleString('en-US'):'—'}
function render(v){
  if(typeof v==='string'){try{v=JSON.parse(v)}catch(e){v=null}}
  var ns=(v&&Array.isArray(v.nights))?v.nights:[];
  var l=document.getElementById('list');
  if(!ns.length){
    l.innerHTML='<div class="k-empty">No nights yet. Each night adds a row here.</div>';
    return;
  }
  var last=ns[ns.length-1];
  document.getElementById('w').textContent=fmt(last.writes)+' writes/s';
  document.getElementById('s').textContent='p99 '+(last.p99!=null?last.p99+' ms':'—')+' · '+(last.night||'')+' · compaction stalls '+(last.stalls!=null?last.stalls:'—');
  l.innerHTML=ns.slice().reverse().map(function(n){
    return '<div class="k-row"><div class="k-row-main">'+(n.night||'')+'</div>'
      +'<div class="k-row-sub">p99 '+(n.p99!=null?n.p99+' ms':'—')+' · stalls '+(n.stalls!=null?n.stalls:'—')+'</div>'
      +'<div class="k-row-value">'+fmt(n.writes)+' /s</div></div>';
  }).join('');
}
var got=ducter.get('nights');
if(got&&typeof got.then==='function'){got.then(render)}else{render(got)}
if(ducter.onChange){ducter.onChange(function(){var g=ducter.get('nights');if(g&&typeof g.then==='function'){g.then(render)}else{render(g)}})}
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"nights":[{"night":"Night 2","writes":181000,"p99":4},{"night":"Night 3","writes":182000,"p99":4.1,"stalls":0}]}

function draw(){
  const xs=ducter.get("expenses")||[];
  const list=document.getElementById("list");
  list.innerHTML="";
  let t=0;
  if(!xs.length){
    const e=document.createElement("div");e.className="k-empty";e.textContent="还没记，点下面「记一笔」";list.appendChild(e);
  }
  for(const x of xs){
    t+=Number(x.yuan)||0;
    const row=document.createElement("div");row.className="k-row";
    const m=document.createElement("div");m.className="k-row-main";m.textContent=x.what;
    const s=document.createElement("div");s.className="k-row-sub";s.textContent=x.day;
    const v=document.createElement("div");v.className="k-row-value";v.textContent="¥"+x.yuan;
    row.appendChild(m);row.appendChild(s);row.appendChild(v);list.appendChild(row);
  }
  const tot=document.getElementById("total");
  if(xs.length){tot.textContent="¥"+t;tot.classList.remove("k-none");}
  else{tot.textContent="还没记";tot.classList.add("k-none");}
  document.getElementById("count").textContent=xs.length?xs.length+" 笔":"记了花销就会算在这里";
  ducter.summary(xs.length?xs.length+" 笔,合计 ¥"+t:"还没记");
}
document.getElementById("add").onclick=function(){
  const what=document.getElementById("what").value.trim();
  const yuan=Number(document.getElementById("yuan").value);
  if(!what||!(yuan>0))return;
  const xs=ducter.get("expenses")||[];
  const day=new Date().toISOString().slice(0,10);
  ducter.set("expenses",xs.concat([{what:what,yuan:yuan,day:day}]));
  document.getElementById("what").value="";
  document.getElementById("yuan").value="";
};
ducter.onChange(draw);draw();
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"expenses":[{"what":"高铁","yuan":662,"day":"2026-11-18"},{"what":"酒店两晚","yuan":1036,"day":"2026-11-18"},{"what":"午饭","yuan":268,"day":"2026-11-19"}]}

  [partner message] Done: "My PRs waiting" is on your shelf with the four PRs. Tap one when it lands to tick it off, and the count at the top updates.
  [an app card under the message: made "My PRs waiting", with Open] the app's file now (v1, 2459 characters): <main class="k-app">
<div class="k-sum">
  <div class="k-sum-label">Waiting on someone</div>
  <div class="k-sum-value" id="count">4</div>
  <div class="k-sum-sub" id="sub">0 landed</div>
</div>
<h2 class="k-h">My PRs</h2>
<div class="k-card"><div class="k-list" id="list"></div></div>
<p class="k-sum-sub" style="margin-top:12px">Tap a PR once it lands to tick it off.</p>
</main>
<script>
var KEY = 'prs';
var DEFAULT = ducter.get("default") || [];
var prs = DEFAULT.map(function(p){ return Object.assign({}, p); });

function save() { ducter.set(KEY, prs); }

function draw() {
  var list = document.getElementById('list');
  list.textContent = '';
  var landed = prs.filter(function(p){ return p.done; }).length;
  var waiting = prs.length - landed;
  document.getElementById('count').textContent = waiting;
  document.getElementById('sub').textContent = landed + ' landed';
  ducter.summary(waiting + ' waiting · ' + landed + ' landed');
  if (!prs.length) {
    var e = document.createElement('div');
    e.className = 'k-empty';
    e.textContent = 'No PRs yet.';
    list.appendChild(e);
    return;
  }
  prs.forEach(function(p, i) {
    var row = document.createElement('button');
    row.type = 'button';
    row.className = 'k-row';
    row.setAttribute('aria-pressed', p.done ? 'true' : 'false');
    row.style.width = '100%';
    row.style.textAlign = 'left';
    row.style.background = 'none';
    row.style.border = '0';
    row.style.color = 'inherit';
    row.style.font = 'inherit';
    row.style.cursor = 'pointer';

    var main = document.createElement('div');
    main.className = 'k-row-main';
    main.textContent = p.repo + ' · ' + p.title;
    if (p.done) main.style.textDecoration = 'line-through';

    var sub = document.createElement('div');
    sub.className = 'k-row-sub';
    sub.textContent = p.note;

    var val = document.createElement('div');
    val.className = 'k-row-value';
    val.textContent = p.done ? '✓ Landed' : '☐';

    row.appendChild(main);
    row.appendChild(sub);
    row.appendChild(val);
    row.addEventListener('click', function() {
      prs[i].done = !prs[i].done;
      save();
      draw();
    });
    list.appendChild(row);
  });
}

Promise.resolve(ducter.get(KEY)).then(function(v) {
  if (Array.isArray(v) && v.length) prs = v;
  draw();
});
ducter.onChange(function() {
  Promise.resolve(ducter.get(KEY)).then(function(v) {
    if (Array.isArray(v)) { prs = v; draw(); }
  });
});
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"prs":[{"id":"ducter-412","repo":"wey-gu/ducter#412","title":"Compaction cut","note":"Waiting on Weizhen's review since Monday","done":false},{"id":"mem-10287","repo":"nowledge-co/mem#10287","title":"Retry on 502","note":"On CI, failing","done":false},{"id":"hawdb-1592","repo":"nowledge-co/hawdb#1592","title":"Flaky compaction test","note":"Waiting on Alice since Tuesday","done":false},{"id":"ducter-418","repo":"wey-gu/ducter#418","title":"Apps bar","note":"Draft","done":false}],"default":[{"id":"ducter-412","repo":"wey-gu/ducter#412","title":"Compaction cut","note":"Waiting on Weizhen's review since Monday","done":false},{"id":"mem-10287","repo":"nowledge-co/mem#10287","title":"Retry on 502","note":"On CI, failing","done":false},{"id":"hawdb-1592","repo":"nowledge-co/hawdb#1592","title":"Flaky compaction test","note":"Waiting on Alice since Tuesday","done":false},{"id":"ducter-418","repo":"wey-gu/ducter#418","title":"Apps bar","note":"Draft","done":false}]}

<h2 class="k-h" id="formtitle">记一笔</h2>
<div class="k-card">
<div class="k-field"><label>出差名称</label><input class="k-input" id="trip" placeholder="比如 北京出差"></div>

<div class="k-chips" id="cats"><button class="k-chip" aria-pressed="true" data-c="交通">交通</button><button class="k-chip" aria-pressed="false" data-c="住宿">住宿</button></div>
<div class="k-field"><label>金额</label><input class="k-num" id="amt" inputmode="decimal" placeholder="0"></div>
<div class="k-field"><label>备注</label><input class="k-input" id="note" placeholder="可不填"></div>
<button class="k-btn primary" id="save">保存</button>
<button class="k-btn" id="del" hidden>删除这笔</button>
</div>
<h2 class="k-h">花销明细</h2>
<div class="k-card"><div class="k-list" id="list"></div></div>
</main>
<script>
const $=id=>document.getElementById(id);
let cat='交通', editing=-1;
const money=n=>'¥'+Number(n||0).toLocaleString('zh-CN',{maximumFractionDigits:2});
const items=()=>ducter. … etForm();draw()};
function draw(){
  const xs=items();
  const total=xs.reduce((s,x)=>s+Number(x.amt||0),0);
  const t=$('total');
  if(xs.length){t.className='k-sum-value';t.textContent=money(total)}else{t.className='k-sum-value k-none';t.textContent='还没记'}
  const trips=[...new Set(xs.map(x=>x.trip).filter(Boolean))];
  $('sub').textContent=xs.length?(xs.length+' 笔'+(trips.length?' · '+trips.join('、'):'')):'记下第一笔就有合计';
  const list=$('list');list.textContent='';
  if(!xs.length){const p=document.createElement('p');p.className='k-empty';p.textContent='还没有花销,记下第一笔吧';list.append(p)}
  xs.forEach((x,i)=>{
    const r=document.createElement('div');r.className='k-row';r.onclick=()=>edit(i);
    const m=document.createElement('div');m.className='k-row-main';m.textContent=(x.cat||'')+(x.trip?' · '+x.trip:'');
    const s=document.createElement('div');s.className='k-row-sub';s.textContent=[x.day,x.note].filter(Boolean).join(' · ');
    const v=document.createElement('div');v.className='k-row-value';v.textContent=money(x.amt);
    r.append(m,s,v);list.append(r);
  });
  ducter.summary(xs.length?(xs.length+' 笔,合计 '+money(total)):'还没记');
}
ducter.onChange(draw);
resetForm();draw();
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"items":[{"trip":"北京出差 11/20–21","day":"2026-11-20","cat":"交通","amt":662,"note":"G1 上海虹桥→北京南 07:00"},{"trip":"北京出差 11/20–21","day":"2026-11-20","cat":"住宿","amt":1036,"note":"亚朵酒店(望京) 两晚"}]}

```

### wp-mem-question-not-fact-en

```
--- step 1: user says: Is pnpm actually faster than npm for a big monorepo? One line.
  [partner message] Usually yes: pnpm's shared content-addressed store and symlinked layout make installs and disk use faster in big monorepos, though the gap has narrowed and it depends on your lockfile and cache state, so benchmark your own repo.

范围：
- 在 Safari 下验证登录页的重定向循环已修好（登录后 next 参数被拒绝、不会回跳到 /login）。
- 补一个测试覆盖这个场景（Safari 下的验证，或能在现有测试框架里稳定复现的等价用例）。
- 完成标准：给出 Safari 验证的步骤和结果，新增测试的通过输出；如果 Safari 环境跑不起来，明确说明原因，不要假装通过。
- 范围外：不要合并、不要推送、不要开 PR，改动留在当前分支上。
- 地点：~/dev/foo，分支 ducter/foo。
